  monthly costs and strong source-clock sensitivity remain. Retain the measured

## September 5, 2026: descriptive S/R + HL encounter follow-up

The [causal encounter study](codex-astra-sr-pulse-encounter-findings-2026-09-05.md)
found 509 spaced encounters, 507 with healthy primary inputs and complete 4h
outcomes. None of eight fixed support/resistance x hold/break x buying/selling
cells passed its advancement gate. Resistance-hold/buying short markouts were
-0.062% after the 0.20% cost scenario (n=46); support-break/selling was -0.214%
(n=22). Sparse controls and unstable temporal results also prevented advancement.

This is **not a new short trading backtest** and does not amend the historical
strategy totals below. The tested descriptive cells are not deployment
candidates; unobserved/thin cells are inconclusive, not universally falsified
mechanisms. Existing live short pause and all trading config remain unchanged.

## September 5, 2026: long sizing and partial-clock audit

[Deep sizing / post-partial exposure findings](codex-astra-ladder-sizing-findings-2026-09-05.md).
Six fixed sizing variants plus cap10 control reduce modeled DD across all four
cases, but none passes the complete profit/protection screens. Reject as tested
qualifying upgrades, not as evidence that sizing cannot reduce risk. This pass
also identifies and fixes a long replay partial-clock mismatch; older long pulse
results retain their old-clock qualification. No short strategy was tested or
enabled by this work, and no live sizing changed.

## Scope

This run covers the Codex shortlist / execution spec only: failed-continuation, spike-rejection, lower-high, composite, BB exhaustion, and VWAP rejection families.

Assumptions:
- Fees: 0.11% round trip taker cost
- Discovery window: 2024-12-05 -> 2025-12-31
- Validation window: 2026-01-01 -> 2026-04-03
- Bear regime: last completed 1H EMA50 < EMA200
- TP/stop simulation uses 5m path data and stop-first ordering for shorts

## 2026-04-25 - Naked BTC dump HYPE add gate

- Hypothesis: BTC leads HYPE dumps by 1-4h, so blocking HYPE ladder entries after BTC 2h ROC <= -0.5% to -2.0% should avoid bad ladders.
- Test: `src/sim-btc-lag-analysis.ts`; HYPE exact episode replay, block rung-1 entry using only last completed BTC 2h ROC.
- Sample: 470 HYPE ladder episodes.
- Result: falsified as a standalone gate. Best all-period threshold was BTC 2h <= -2% and still lost -1206 USD; threshold <= -1% blocked 27 episodes, caught 0 bad episodes, and cost 5249 USD.
- Mechanism: HYPE often rebounds after BTC dump hours in this dataset; BTC dump is context, not a delayed-crash predictor by itself.

## Codex 5.10 SOL Strategy-Class Sweep (2026-04-26T21:26:28.457Z)

## Codex 5.11 Portfolio Sim (2026-04-27T07:40Z)

- Scope: shared-margin HYPE + wed-short + d1-short + PF0 + SOL portfolio replay with Bybit risk tiers, funding, mark-to-market drawdown, and margin-block/forced-liquidation accounting.
- Result: do not ship SOL from this pass. Current HYPE $1200 + best SOL $300 improves return only +15.3 percentage points on $25k, below the +30 point ship bar.
- Best top-line row was HYPE base $1800 + SOL $300, but HYPE-only $1800 already returned +1238.5%; adding SOL lifted that to +1253.8% while worsening MaxDD from 49.4% to 50.3%.
- Stress blocker: November 2025 replay drops combined min equity to 22.4% of starting equity, failing the required 50% survival floor.
- Carry-forward rule: evaluate HYPE base scale-up separately; do not credit SOL for returns mostly created by scaling the HYPE ladder.

## HYPE HL Short-System Expansion (2026-07-16)

- Scope: 59.17-day common HYPE/Hyperliquid microstructure window (2026-05-17 20:45 UTC to 2026-07-16 00:45 UTC), 5,681 strict 15m decisions. All pulse rows are source timestamp `< T`; entry is the next 1m open; stop-first; 0.11% fee base / 0.20% stress; first-half exit selection and frozen second-half evaluation.
- FORWARD SHADOW: `hl_bid_pull_break` — HL 0.5% book imbalance deteriorates by >0.15 from prior-10m to last-5m, last-5m imbalance < -0.05, HL 15m taker ratio <0.90, and a completed 15m downside break. Frozen TP2% / SL4% / 12h: train n=16, +0.916% expectancy; test n=20, +1.153%; test at 0.20% fees +1.063%; one-minute delayed test at 0.20% fees +0.810%. Monthly expectancy: May +0.521% (n=7), June +1.182% (n=22), July +1.152% (n=7).
- ABLATION: plain completed breaks were -0.094% (n=612); breaks + HL taker selling were -0.103% (n=559); HL book withdrawal alone was -0.061% (n=196). Edge appeared only at the three-way intersection.
- FALSIFIED on expanded window: old 5.15b Bybit bid-pull + volume candidate (second half -0.036%, fee stress -0.126%); OI/funding crowded-long breaks; OI-price divergence; liquidation blow-off; generic trend-break selling; all tested adverse-add short-ladder variants.
- LOW_N carry-forward: `session-dailyred-wick` remained positive in this window (n=8, +1.051% selected-exit expectancy) but is below the sample gate.
- Carry-forward rule: exact live-decision shadow for 30–60 days before any deployment discussion. A future HYPE short must share one transactional hedge-side owner with existing HYPE short paths; no independent executor/process ownership.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-system-findings-2026-07-16.md`.

## HYPE HL Short Architecture Study (2026-07-16)

- Scope: frozen `hl_bid_pull_break` signal, 42 raw fires over the same strict 59.18-day window. Tested 460 predeclared single, parallel-slot, adverse, favorable, hybrid, and repeat-confirmation structures with first-half selection, second-half evaluation, 0.20%/0.30% fees, one-minute delay, fixed total reserved-capital accounting, and conservative stop-first/add-candle handling.
- PROVEN BASELINE: one full initial short with TP 2%, SL 4%, 12h remains best per reserved capital: +13.219% train, +21.252% test, +34.472% total at 0.20% fees; all three calendar partitions positive.
- FALSIFIED AS PROFIT MAXIMIZERS: train-selected adverse (+28.333% all), favorable (+23.482%), hybrid (+27.261%), repeat-confirmation (+24.850%), equal-capital two-slot parallel (+19.252%), and 50/50 baseline/adverse (+31.403%) all underperform baseline. Adverse adds improve on leaving 30% idle but lose to deploying the same capital at the signal.
- LOW_N FORWARD CANDIDATE: a genuinely additional second independent slot captured six serially skipped exact signals and added +0.991% train / +3.041% test / +4.032% all at 0.20% fees. Still +3.432% at 0.30% costs and +4.501% with one-minute delay, but n=6 is below proof. Shadow only; no live orders.
- Carry-forward rule: do not replace the frozen single shadow with a dynamic ladder. Add only a separately versioned read-only overlap-slot counterfactual, and require at least 15 total overlap trades plus positive forward/stress results before deployment discussion.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-architecture-findings-2026-07-16.md`.

## HYPE HL Short Shared-Account Replay (2026-07-16)

- Scope: current HYPE long ladder unchanged, including exact S/R partial exits and support reopens, overlaid with the frozen short ledger on one `$32k` account. Minute mark-to-market, configured fees, Bybit funding, 25x initial margin, and local public HYPE maintenance tiers included.
- PORTFOLIO PASS: one `$800` frozen short slot adds **+$302.55** after fees/funding to long-only **+$25,513.01**, producing **+$25,815.56** combined. Increment is positive in train/test and May/June/July; 0.20% short-cost stress still adds **+$276.78**.
- DIVERSIFICATION: combined MaxDD improves from **16.385% to 16.307%**; active minute PnL correlation is **-0.814**. 32/36 signals occur with a long open and 29/36 at long depth >=5. Minimum free margin remains **$28,783.99** and no margin/liquidation path binds.
- LOW_N SECOND SLOT: two `$800` slots add only **+$37.08** beyond the first, from six overlap trades. Keep the second slot shadow-only under its `n>=15 overlap` gate.
- Carry-forward rule: historical financial coexistence passes, but no live order enablement from this research pass. Require the 30–60 day forward cohort and a single crash-tested transactional HYPE short-side owner. The follow-up notional frontier supersedes the matched-base `$800` diagnostic: `$25k` is the balanced design target and `$30k` the aggressive ceiling.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-shared-account-findings-2026-07-16.md`.

## HYPE HL Short Notional Frontier (2026-07-16)

- Scope: shared-account short-notional sweep every `$500` through `$60k`, with current long stack unchanged, exact MTM, funding/margin, 0.11%/0.20%/0.30% costs, one-minute delay, and strict-prior Bybit entry-depth joins.
- PORTFOLIO RESULT: every requested `$5k–$35k` size improved historical MaxDD versus long-only 16.385%. `$25k` added +$9,455 exact / +$7,755 at 0.30% costs with 14.67% worst-case DD; train/test and May/June/July stayed positive.
- LIQUIDITY: all 36 `$35k` entries fit inside displayed strict-prior 0.1% Bybit bid depth; minimum 0.1% depth was `$82,950`. `$25k` consumed at most about 30.1% of that band.
- SIZING VERDICT: `$22k` strict-comfort (heavy-cost stop <=3% equity), `$25k` balanced recommendation, `$30k` aggressive ceiling (heavy-cost stop ~4.04%). `$35k` passes history but is deferred until real execution evidence because a stopped trade costs ~4.71% of starting equity.
- Carry-forward rule: do not select the in-sample `$34.5k` minimum-DD point. Validate one `$25k` primary short owner before considering `$30k`; no second slot or short ladder.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-notional-frontier-findings-2026-07-16.md`.

## HYPE HL Short Exit Mitigation (2026-07-16)

- Scope: 121 predeclared exit policies over the frozen 42-signal / 36-serial-trade cohort. Families cover 2.5–6% stops, 4–24h holds, age/loss cuts, MFE failure, completed-candle reclaim, HL flow reversal, break-even, trailing, and partial profits. Selection is first-half only; gates include second half, 0.30% cost, one-minute delay, and monthly stability.
- FALSIFIED AS IMPROVEMENTS: no family winner passed. Break-even and trailing eliminate visible stops but lose -6.246% and -6.645% of one notional unit full-window. Closing 25% at +1% improves worst trade to -2.95% but costs -2.462%, about -$615 at $25k.
- STOP/TIMEOUT RESULT: wider 4.5–6% stops do not improve the train-selected family; shorter 4–10h holds are materially worse. The exact-path 16h hold adds +1.485% but fails delay (-3.768%) and calendar (-2.020%) gates.
- Carry-forward rule: retain TP2% / SL4% / 12h unchanged. Do not add break-even, trailing, partial profit, wider stop, or adaptive timeout from this cohort. Build a separate crash-tested transactional short owner before any live discussion; the current generic hedge helpers are not safe for a `$25k` strategy position.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-exit-mitigation-findings-2026-07-16.md`.

## HYPE HL Short Final-Hour Timeout Replay (2026-08-10)

- Scope: frozen `hl_bid_pull_break` over 43 completed serial trades, including nine timeouts and seven completed post-live forward trades. Tested 40 causal rules that search hours 11-12 using trailing 8h/11h price rank, distance from the trailing low, or an observed final-hour trough/rebound. The still-active August 9 short was excluded from every policy.
- FALSIFIED FOR LIVE USE: no rule was positive in both train and the prior untouched test. The train-selected 11h/bottom-15% rule adds +1.182% full-window (about +$296 at $25k) but loses -0.356% in prior test and changes no completed forward trade. The train-selected rebound rule adds +0.398% forward but loses -1.580% in prior test and only +0.133% overall.
- LOOKAHEAD BOUND: the nine timeout windows contain an unattainable +4.955% / about $1,239 best-minute opportunity. The causal selectors cannot capture it reliably; July 30's local trough remained in the expensive 70.76th percentile of its preceding 11h, while August 8's fixed timeout was already the best final-hour executable mark.
- Carry-forward rule: retain the fixed 12h close. At most shadow the 11h/bottom-15% counterfactual until 10-15 new completed timeouts span multiple regimes; do not promote ordinary-trade count as timeout evidence.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-timeout-window-findings-2026-08-10.md`.

## HYPE HL Short Nearby-TP Replay (2026-08-10)

- Scope: frozen short with full-position TP swept from 1.50% to 2.50% in uniform 0.05-point steps over 43 completed historical trades, plus a receipt-based counterfactual over seven completed live shorts.
- APPROVED LIVE POLICY V2: TP 1.95% is train-selected and adds +4.400% / about +$1,100 exact full-window. It converts one May trade that reached 1.979% MFE before stopping. One-minute-delay full delta remains +1.637% / about +$409, but the cohort signs move from exact train/test/forward `+5.30/-0.70/-0.20%` to delayed `-0.70/+0.598/+1.738%`.
- ACTUAL-FILL SUPPORT: a 1.95% native TP would have converted the July 31 live timeout from -$16.40 to about +$460.42. Across all seven completed live shorts, the counterfactual is -$61.16 versus actual -$520.20, a +$459.03 delta after the small premium paid on existing TP winners.
- FALSIFIED: every target above 2% materially underperforms; 2.05% alone loses -11.870% versus baseline. Targets at or below 1.90% are less efficient versions of the same near-miss insurance and/or fail aggregate expectancy.
- Carry-forward rule: deploy TP1.95 only while the short owner is flat, under a new policy version/signature. Judge ongoing performance by net dollar delta, not win rate; the mechanism intentionally gives up 0.05% on ordinary winners to catch rare near-target reversals.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-nearby-tp-findings-2026-08-10.md`.

## HYPE HL Short Regime Revalidation (2026-09-04)

- Scope: expanded the frozen TP1.95% / SL4% / 12h serial replay through 2026-09-03 23:45 UTC using 51 historical fires plus 21 production-shadow fires (8 overlaps, 64 unique raw signals). Exact and one-minute-delay paths use only completed causal bars, stop-first exits, and 0.20% stress costs.
- FORWARD FAILURE: the 43-trade old-evidence cohort remains +38.295% stress, but the genuinely untouched cohort is n=14 and -20.544% exact (n=13 and -10.441% delayed). Expanded full history remains positive at +17.750% exact / +24.617% delayed, so this is deterioration rather than proof that the original edge never existed.
- FALSIFIED AS REGIME FIXES: original down-regime/local-down allowlists, bull-alignment suspensions, fixed EMA200-extension thresholds, seven-day rally thresholds, composites, and a persistent rally latch. No predeclared rule preserved old/monthly performance while improving untouched exact and delayed paths. The closest extension+rally+pullback composite is concentrated in the Aug 24 cluster and misses the September timeout/stop.
- LIVE CONFIRMATION: 20 actual completed shorts are -$3,891.07 net; the same opened signal IDs replay near -$3,780 configured / -$4,230 stress. Execution is not the primary cause.
- Carry-forward rule: pause new live short entries while flat, retain the transactional owner/shadows/watchdog, and do not deploy a fitted regime gate from this pass. Reconsider only from a newly frozen hypothesis and fresh untouched evidence.
- Full findings: `research/codex-hl-short-regime-revalidation-findings-2026-09-04.md`.

## Long-only exposure-control cross-reference (2026-09-05)

- This is **not a short test**; short entries remain paused. Seventeen frozen
  long add-veto variants ran through both repaired-baseline windows and both TP
  assumptions: 68 variant cases, four exact baseline digests.
- NOT QUALIFIED AS ROBUST UPGRADES: none passes the complete predeclared profit
  or defensive-trade-off screen. Simple drop-only and broad S/R results change
  materially across TP models/history; sparse flow/weak-context conjunctions
  either barely intervene or do not meaningfully reduce the large losses.
- REAL DEFENSIVE TRADE-OFF, NOT FREE ALPHA: cap10 cuts HL gross losing-episode
  PnL by 27.2% / 24.0% and DD by 7.76 / 6.13pp, costing $1,242 / $1,145 net.
  Published resting-touch loses $13,323 and fails retention/monthly-cost limits.
- The strict weak + resistance + selling timer conjunction never intervenes;
  this is ineffective on the tested history, not evidence that every possible
  pulse gate is falsified. Pulse vetoes on genuine drop adds were not tested.
- No live changes or forward promotion. Full ranking, top-five monthly deltas,
  causal trace, original/revised source provenance and limitations:
  [exposure-control findings](codex-astra-ladder-exposure-controls-findings-2026-09-05.md).

## Long-only genuine-drop pulse follow-up (2026-09-05)

- Not a short test. Twelve frozen HL taker confirmation/veto rules at depth 11
  or >=9, genuine price-drop adds only; 48 full-path cases, four exact repaired
  baseline digests. Engine and live config unchanged.
- REJECTED AS TESTED BLEEDING FIXES: broad depth >=9 selling vetoes and positive
  confirmation sacrifice recovery/cycling and worsen aggregate risk. Requiring
  taker15m >=1 loses $13,159 / $12,292 in the two HL TP models, with 29 / 28 fewer
  TP cycles and 17.89 / 11.72pp worse DD. n=93 / 92 vetoed episodes, not sparse.
- QUALIFIED POSITIVE, NOT RISK PROTECTION: rung-11-only taker15m <=0.85 veto is
  +$366 / +$3,074 in HL and +$406 / +$2,868 in published controls, but both HL
  drawdowns worsen and gross losses do not consistently improve. Do not claim
  its profit effect falsified; it fails the protective objective/screens.
- S/R restriction does not rescue the tested gates. All 12 miss both complete
  predeclared screens; no variant case reduces its baseline forced-close count.
- Timer-only releases and leaving the resistance band often end holds before
  positive-flow confirmation. Persistent gates, different S/R geometry and other
  pulse families were not tested. No live or forward promotion.
- Full rankings, monthly deltas, release diagnostics and causal raw-stream trace:
  [genuine-drop pulse findings](codex-astra-ladder-drop-pulse-findings-2026-09-05.md).

## Long-only persistent pulse-hold follow-up (2026-09-05)

- Six frozen holds (sell15, confirm_both, sr_confirm at 11 or >=9): 24 full-path
  cases, four exact baselines, and paired comparison to hashed prior stateless
  controls. No live changes; not a short test.
- REJECTED AS ROBUST RISK UPGRADES: persistence does not rescue these rules.
  Strict depth >=9 hold is -$17,824 / -$6,098 versus HL baseline; persistence
  itself is -$9,113 / +$6,815 versus the same old rule. S/R depth >=9 worsens
  both compared with the stateless version. 0/6 passes either declared screen.
- MILD POSITIVE RETAINED: sell15 at rung 11 is +$459 / +$3,084 versus HL baseline,
  but persistence only adds $92 / $10, both HL drawdowns worsen and gross losses
  do not consistently improve. Not promoted as bleeding reduction.
- Every gate transition and actual open was verified; timer/zone permissions
  really were prevented. No variant reduces forced-close count. No threshold
  retuning or live promotion. Different sizing/notional controls remain untested.
- Side observation: one S/R-gated path raises peak surviving entry-cost notional
  from $69,768 to $80,748. Add eligibility is not a hard dollar exposure cap after
  partial exits; not a claim of a production defect or a proven cap solution.
- Full paired/monthly tables and causal state trace:
  [persistent pulse findings](codex-astra-ladder-persistent-pulse-findings-2026-09-05.md).

## CRSI standalone risk/exit follow-up I03 (2026-09-06)

- Long-only, not a new short test. Two frozen 15m oversold-long entries into <=5
  and recovery >5; compare original 12h with separate observed 3/5/8% stops,
  6h timeout, or CRSI >=50/>=80 exits capped at 12h. Twelve new definitions;
  eight old baselines exact; 56 cases/5,256 trade rows independently verified.
- Same I01/I02 full 2025-07-01..2026-09-04 19:01 UTC and contained recent
  2026-05-17 20:43..cutoff, $10k fixed notional, 0.055% each side, 0/+1m actions,
  funding excluded. **All 48 variant cases have lower own-baseline net.**
- NOT SUPPORTED AS UPGRADES UNDER THESE EXACT RULES: 0/12 passes profit
  or defensive screens. Recovery 8% stop gives full +$7,852 vs +$8,684,
  DD 9.64% vs 8.68%; recent +$6,071 vs +$6,272. Early indicator exits often
  improve loser outcomes but sacrifice substantially more winner profit.
- POSITIVE ORIGINAL ENTRY EVIDENCE RETAINED: no blanket CRSI rejection.
  Original into/recovery 12h rules remain profitable in both windows/delays;
  they still have I02 monthly opportunity-cost and execution/margin caveats.
  Observed stops avoid the later October crash low, but do not guarantee a
  percentage loss cap and do not improve the aggregate frozen strategy.
- No live change, new entry threshold, combined exit or native stop fill
  certification. Bounded CRSI follow-up ends here; RSI individual coverage next,
  then other scoped families before indicator/HL/S/R combinations.
- All rankings, baseline W/L dollars, four-case monthly tables and causal exit trace:
